# Assumptions register

Generated by `scripts/build_assumptions_register.py` from `utilities/*.json`; do not edit by hand. The full register, with sources, as-of dates and notes in full, is [`assumptions_register.csv`](assumptions_register.csv). `tests/test_assumptions_register.py` fails if either file is out of date with the JSON.

Status: **sourced** = read from a cited document; **derived** = calculated from sourced figures; **_VERIFY** = estimate, assumption or proxy awaiting confirmation; **setting** = a model switch or mode choice. Ranges are low / central / high; scenarios and the tornado pick levels (see [METHODOLOGY.md](METHODOLOGY.md)).
